Excluding (from config): build, dist
  Cluster: error-handling
  Type: Manual
  Description: Fix broken error handling: SystemExit(string) bugs, bare except blocks swallowing failures, wrong exception types (SystemExit vs CommandError)
  Steps (10):
    1. Read each file at the listed line number to understand what the catch block is catching and what's being suppressed
    2. Replace 'except X: log.warning(...)' with one of: (a) re-raise using 'raise CommandError(msg) from e' for CLI-facing catches, (b) 'logger.exception(msg)' (not warning) to capture full traceback, or (c) return a typed failure value when callers expect Optional/Result
    3. cli.py:88 — this is the top-level CLI handler: re-raise as CommandError so the CLI framework can format and exit cleanly
    4. engine/_plan/persistence.py:92 — plan load failure: log.exception() and return an empty/default plan; do NOT silently continue with corrupt state
    5. app/commands/scan/artifacts.py:84, scan/preflight.py:45, scan/plan_reconcile.py:334, scan/reporting/summary.py:294 — scan pipeline: log.exception() with context ('failed to X during scan') then re-raise or return sentinel so caller aborts cleanly
    6. app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py:553 — plan triage handler: log.exception() + return early; never silently continue after a triage mutation failure
    7. languages/_framework/treesitter/__init__.py:19, treesitter/_import_cache.py:34, languages/_framework/discovery.py:111 — lang plugin init: log.exception() is acceptable here since plugin failures are non-fatal, but add the module name to the message
    8. intelligence/review/context_holistic/selection_contexts.py:119,131, intelligence/review/prepare.py:421 — review context: these need to raise so callers know a review packet is incomplete; change to re-raise
    9. app/output/scorecard_parts/meta.py:65,74 — git subprocess: log.exception() is fine, but also log the full stderr from the subprocess to aid debugging
    10. Run: python -m pytest desloppify/tests/ -q after all changes to verify no regressions

These add a layer of indirection with no benefit, obscure the real API, and accumulate technical debt. Fix: find all callers, update them to call the underlying function directly, delete the wrapper.
  Steps (8):
    1. For each function: run 'grep -rn "function_name(" desloppify/' to find every call site before touching anything
    2. _coerce_generic_lang_options in languages/_framework/generic.py (7/8 forwarded): read the function, identify the 1 arg it doesn't forward (likely a default or transformation) — if it truly adds nothing, delete it and update the callers. If the 1 arg differs, inline that logic at each call site instead.
    3. _coerce_import_load_config in app/commands/review/importing/helpers.py (7/8 forwarded): same process — read it, find callers with 'grep -rn _coerce_import_load_config desloppify/', then inline the underlying call at each site
    4. _execute_parallel in app/commands/review/_runner_parallel_execution.py (7/8 forwarded): this is in the parallel review runner — read carefully since parallel execution context may have subtle state. Find the underlying function it calls and verify callers can safely call it directly.

> AGENT

Perfect! I've captured all 12 cluster outputs in full. Here's a summary of the auto clusters in the desloppify plan:

## Summary of Auto Clusters

**1. auto/smells-noop_function** (8 issues)
- Non-trivial functions with empty bodies across rendering and scanning modules

**2. auto/smells-high_cyclomatic_complexity** (35 issues)
- Functions with >12 decision points spread throughout commands (next, plan, review, scan), engine modules (_plan, _scoring, _state, _work_queue), and supporting code

**3. auto/smells-deferred_import** (31 issues)
- Function-level imports (possible circular import workarounds) scattered across app commands, engine, intelligence, and language framework modules

**4. auto/smells-duplicate_constant** (2 issues)
- Identical constants in `/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py` and `/engine/_plan/cluster_strategy.py`

**5. auto/smells-mutable_class_var** (5 issues)
- Class-level mutable defaults mostly in test files (TypeScript tests and command resolve/detect/move tests)

**6. auto/smells-optional_param_sprawl** (7 issues)
- Functions with too many optional parameters in review importing, plan operations, detectors, and framework

**7. auto/smells-annotation_quality** (15 issues / 14 members)
- Loose type annotations (any/etc) in resolve, review, scoring, output, base discovery, and language framework

**8. auto/security** (56 issues)
- Bandit security issues dominated by subprocess calls (B603, B607), XML parsing vulnerabilities, MD5 weak hashing, and URL handling
- Spread across: app commands, output, base git context, engine, and language detectors/extractors

### 1. ERROR-HANDLING (13 items)
**Description:** Fix broken error handling: SystemExit(string) bugs, bare except blocks swallowing failures, wrong exception types

**Problem Areas:**
- `cli.py:88` — top-level CLI handler needs to re-raise as CommandError
- `engine/_plan/persistence.py:92` — plan load failure should log.exception() and return empty plan, not silently continue
- `app/commands/scan/artifacts.py:84`, `scan/preflight.py:45`, `scan/plan_reconcile.py:334`, `scan/reporting/summary.py:294` — scan pipeline exceptions should log.exception() then re-raise
- `app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py:553` — plan triage handler should log.exception() + return early
- `languages/_framework/treesitter/__init__.py:19`, `treesitter/_import_cache.py:34`, `languages/_framework/discovery.py:111` — plugin init failures acceptable with log.exception() but need module name in message
- `intelligence/review/context_holistic/selection_contexts.py:119,131`, `intelligence/review/prepare.py:421` — review context needs to raise, not silently fail
- `app/output/scorecard_parts/meta.py:65,74` — git subprocess should also log full stderr

### 2. **Code Smells** (3 clusters)

- **auto/smells-monster_function**: 8 issues (10 functions >150 LOC)
  - Spread across unrelated modules (parser_groups, execution, detectors, etc.)
  - **Assessment**: **Mixed — some are tool artifacts.** The parser_groups files are generated/framework boilerplate. The others (execution, flat_dirs, generic) are real.

- **auto/smells-swallowed_error**: 13 issues
  - All are deliberate logging-only catches in error recovery paths
  - Examples: `plan_reconcile.py`, `scan/artifacts.py`, `cli.py`
  - **Assessment**: **Mostly noise** — these catches log errors intentionally. Framework code sometimes needs graceful degradation.

- **auto/smells-silent_except**: 2 issues  
  - `runner_parallel.py::105` — parallel execution cleanup (acceptable)
  - `unused_enums.py::33,37` — detector retries on parse failure (acceptable)
  - **Assessment**: **Noise** — these are deliberate suppression patterns for robustness.

---

### 3. **Import Cycles** (auto/cycles)

3 real import cycles:
1. `schema.py ↔ schema_scores.py` — state persistence coupling
2. `plan_reconcile.py ↔ workflow.py` — scan orchestration circular dependency
3. `typescript/__init__.py ↔ _fixers.py` — language plugin initialization

**Assessment**: **Real problems** — these are fixable and worth untangling.

---

### 4. **Facade Files** (auto/facade-file)

21 files that are pure re-export wrappers:
- Language `_review.py` facades (python, typescript, csharp)
- Test integration facades (5 files in `tests/review/integration/`)
- Various `__init__.py` re-exports

**Assessment**: **This contradicts CLAUDE.md policy.** The backward compatibility policy says: *"No backward compatibility for import paths or APIs... Remove re-export facades, wrapper shims, and compatibility layers."* These 21 files are exactly that.

---

### 5. **Directory Overload** (auto/flat_dirs-overload)

7 directories with too many child items:
- `desloppify/languages` — 1 file + 29 child dirs
- `desloppify/app/commands` — 12 files + 10 child dirs
- `desloppify/engine/_plan` — 24 files, 0 subdirs (the biggest concern)
- Test directories with 20+ files each

**Assessment**: **Partially valid.** The `_plan` directory (24 files, no hierarchy) is a real organizational problem. The others could be addressed through grouping, but the feedback is less actionable.

- **auto/responsibility_cohesion-render.py**: 2 files with 9-10 functions each across 6 disconnected clusters
- **auto/responsibility_cohesion-scope.py**: 2 files with 10-11 functions across 5-6 disconnected clusters

1→"""Large file detection (LOC threshold)."""
     2→
     3→import logging
     4→from pathlib import Path
     5→
     6→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
     7→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import count_lines, resolve_scan_file
     8→
     9→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    10→
    11→
    12→def detect_large_files(
    13→    path: Path, file_finder, threshold: int = 500
    14→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    15→    """Find files exceeding a line count threshold."""
    16→    files = file_finder(path)
    17→    entries = []
    18→    for filepath in files:
    19→        try:
    20→            p = resolve_scan_file(filepath, scan_root=path)
    21→            loc = count_lines(p)
    22→            if loc > threshold:
    23→                entries.append({"file": filepath, "loc": loc})
    24→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
    25→            log_best_effort_failure(
    26→                logger,
    27→                f"read large-file detector candidate {filepath}",
    28→                exc,
    29→            )
    30→            continue
    31→    return sorted(entries, key=lambda e: -e["loc"]), len(files)
    32→

<system-reminder>

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>
```

1→"""God class/component detection via configurable rule-based analysis."""
     2→
     3→
     4→def detect_gods(classes, rules, min_reasons: int = 2) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
     5→    """Find god classes/components — entities with too many responsibilities."""
     6→    entries = []
     7→    for cls in classes:
     8→        reasons = []
     9→        for rule in rules:
    10→            value = rule.extract(cls)
    11→            if value >= rule.threshold:
    12→                reasons.append(f"{value} {rule.description}")
    13→
    14→        if len(reasons) >= min_reasons:
    15→            entries.append(
    16→                {
    17→                    "file": cls.file,
    18→                    "name": cls.name,
    19→                    "loc": cls.loc,
    20→                    "reasons": reasons,
    21→                    "signal_text": f"{cls.name} ({', '.join(reasons[:2])})",
    22→                    "detail": {**cls.metrics, "name": cls.name},
    23→                }
    24→            )
    25→    return sorted(entries, key=lambda e: -e["loc"]), len(classes)
    26→

   137→def _render_dimension_context(detector: str, dim_scores: dict) -> None:

   290→def _render_item(

   318→def _item_label(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> str:

   323→        return f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}]"
   324→    pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   325→    return f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   326→
   327→
   328→def render_terminal_items(
   329→    items: list[dict],
   330→    dim_scores: dict,
   331→    issues_scoped: dict,
   332→    *,
   333→    group: str,
   334→    explain: bool,
   335→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   336→    plan: dict | None = None,
   337→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   338→) -> None:
   339→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   340→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   341→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   342→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   343→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   344→        total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   345→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   346→
   347→    if group != "item":
   348→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   349→        return
   350→
   351→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   352→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   353→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   354→    )
   355→
   356→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   357→        if idx > 0:
   358→            print()
   359→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   360→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   361→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   362→            continue
   363→        label = _item_label(item, idx, len(items))
   364→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   365→        _render_item(
   366→            item, dim_scores, issues_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials,
   367→            single_item=len(items) == 1,
   368→        )
   369→
   370→
   371→__all__ = [
   372→    "render_terminal_items",
   373→]
   374→

1→"""Pure grouping/description/action strategy for plan auto-clustering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DetectorMeta
     8→
     9→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    10→
    11→
    12→def extract_subtype(issue: dict) -> str | None:
    13→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a issue."""
    14→    detail = issue.get("detail") or {}
    15→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    16→    if kind:
    17→        return kind
    18→
    19→    issue_id = issue.get("id", "")
    20→    parts = issue_id.split("::")
    21→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    22→        candidate = parts[-1]
    23→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    24→            return candidate
    25→    return None
    26→
    27→
    28→def grouping_key(issue: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    29→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a issue."""
    30→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    31→
    32→    if meta is None:
    33→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    34→
    35→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    36→        detail = issue.get("detail") or {}
    37→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    38→        if dimension:
    39→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    40→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    41→
    42→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in ("structural", "responsibility_cohesion"):
    43→        file_path = issue.get("file", "")
    44→        if file_path:
    45→            basename = os.path.basename(file_path)
    46→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    47→
    48→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    49→        subtype = extract_subtype(issue)
    50→        if subtype:
    51→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    52→
    53→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    54→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    55→
    56→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    57→
    58→
    59→def cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    60→    """Convert a grouping key to an ``auto/...`` cluster name."""
    61→    parts = key.split("::")
    62→    if len(parts) == 2:
    63→        prefix_type = parts[0]
    64→        if prefix_type == "review":
    65→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
    66→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
    67→    if len(parts) == 3:
    68→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
    69→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
    70→
    71→
    72→def generate_description(
    73→    cluster_name: str,
    74→    members: list[dict],
    75→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
    76→    subtype: str | None,
    77→) -> str:
    78→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
    79→    _ = cluster_name
    80→    count = len(members)
    81→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
    82→
    83→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    84→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
    85→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
    86→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review issues"
    87→
    88→    if detector == "structural":
    89→        files = {os.path.basename(member.get("file", "")) for member in members}
    90→        if len(files) == 1:
    91→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
    92→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
    93→
    94→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
    95→    if subtype:
    96→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
    97→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
    98→
    99→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   100→        return f"Remove {count} {display} issues"
   101→
   102→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   103→
   104→
   105→def subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   106→    """Check if a subtype maps to a detector fixer."""
   107→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   108→        return None
   109→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   110→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   111→        return fixer_name
   112→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   113→        if subtype in fixer:
   114→            return fixer
   115→    return None
   116→
   117→
   118→def strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   119→    """Strip subtype examples from guidance text."""
   120→    if " — " in guidance:
   121→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   122→    return guidance
   123→
   124→
   125→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   126→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   127→    "refactor": "review and refactor each issue",
   128→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each issue",
   129→}
   130→
   131→
   132→def generate_action(
   133→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   134→    subtype: str | None,
   135→) -> str:
   136→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata."""
   137→    if meta is None:
   138→        return "review and fix each issue"
   139→
   140→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   141→        matched_fixer = subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   142→        if matched_fixer:
   143→            return f"desloppify autofix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   144→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   145→        return f"desloppify autofix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   146→
   147→    if meta.tool == "move":
   148→        return "desloppify move"
   149→
   150→    if meta.guidance:
   151→        if subtype:
   152→            return strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   153→        return meta.guidance
   154→
   155→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(meta.action_type, "review and fix each issue")
   156→
   157→
   158→__all__ = [
   159→    "cluster_name_from_key",
   160→    "generate_action",
   161→    "generate_description",
   162→    "grouping_key",
   163→    "strip_guidance_examples",
   164→]
   165→

    68→def compute_narrative(
    69→    state: StateModel,
    70→    context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
    71→) -> NarrativeResult:
    72→    """Compute structured narrative context from state data."""
    73→    resolved_context = context or NarrativeContext(command="")
    74→

    77→    command = resolved_context.command
    78→    config = resolved_context.config

    97→        by_detector=by_detector,

   100→        debt=debt,
   101→        lang=lang,

     3→Separate module to avoid import cycles — submodules import from here
     4→instead of from __init__.py.

     7→from __future__ import annotations

    19→def _refresh_detector_tools() -> None:
    20→    """Rebuild DETECTOR_TOOLS from current DETECTORS."""
    21→    DETECTOR_TOOLS.clear()
    22→    DETECTOR_TOOLS.update(_detector_tools())

1→"""Tool inventory helpers for narrative payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_engine import supported_fixers
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ToolFixer, ToolInventory
    10→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    11→
    12→
    13→def _move_reasons(by_detector: dict[str, int]) -> list[str]:
    14→    reasons: list[str] = []
    15→    if by_detector.get("orphaned", 0):
    16→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['orphaned']} orphaned files")
    17→    if by_detector.get("coupling", 0):
    18→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['coupling']} coupling violations")
    19→    if by_detector.get("single_use", 0):
    20→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['single_use']} single-use files")
    21→    if by_detector.get("flat_dirs", 0):
    22→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['flat_dirs']} flat directories")
    23→    if by_detector.get("naming", 0):
    24→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['naming']} naming issues")
    25→    return reasons
    26→
    27→
    28→def _build_fixers(
    29→    by_detector: dict[str, int], state: StateModel, lang: str | None
    30→) -> list[ToolFixer]:
    31→    fixers: list[ToolFixer] = []
    32→    supported = supported_fixers(state, lang)
    33→
    34→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    35→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
    36→            continue
    37→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
    38→        if count == 0:
    39→            continue
    40→
    41→        for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]:
    42→            if supported is not None and fixer not in supported:
    43→                continue
    44→            fixers.append(
    45→                {
    46→                    "name": fixer,
    47→                    "detector": detector,
    48→                    "open_count": count,
    49→                    "command": f"desloppify autofix {fixer} --dry-run",
    50→                }
    51→            )
    52→
    53→    return fixers
    54→
    55→
    56→def compute_tools(
    57→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    58→    state: StateModel,
    59→    lang: str | None,
    60→    badge: dict[str, Any],
    61→) -> ToolInventory:
    62→    move_keys = ["orphaned", "flat_dirs", "naming", "single_use", "coupling", "cycles"]
    63→    organizational_issues = sum(by_detector.get(detector, 0) for detector in move_keys)
    64→    reasons = _move_reasons(by_detector)
    65→
    66→    return {
    67→        "fixers": _build_fixers(by_detector, state, lang),
    68→        "move": {
    69→            "available": True,
    70→            "relevant": organizational_issues > 0,
    71→            "reason": " + ".join(reasons) if reasons else None,
    72→            "usage": "desloppify move <source> <dest> [--dry-run]",
    73→        },
    74→        "plan": {
    75→            "command": "desloppify plan",
    76→            "description": "Generate prioritized markdown cleanup plan",
    77→        },
    78→        "badge": badge,
    79→    }

1→"""Contextual reminders with decay."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import logging
     6→from datetime import UTC
     7→from datetime import datetime as _dt
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
    11→    _FEEDBACK_URL,
    12→    _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD,
    13→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE,
    14→)

    20→def _compute_fp_rates(issues: dict) -> dict[tuple[str, str], float]:
    21→    """Compute false_positive rate per (detector, zone) from historical issues."""
    22→    counts: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, int]] = {}
    23→    for issue in issues.values():
    24→        detector = issue.get("detector", "unknown")
    25→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    26→            detector = "structural"
    27→        zone = issue.get("zone", "production")
    28→        key = (detector, zone)
    29→        if key not in counts:
    30→            counts[key] = {"total": 0, "fp": 0}
    31→        counts[key]["total"] += 1
    32→        if issue.get("status") == "false_positive":
    33→            counts[key]["fp"] += 1
    34→
    35→    rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = {}
    36→    for key, stat in counts.items():
    37→        if stat["total"] >= 5 and stat["fp"] > 0:
    38→            rates[key] = stat["fp"] / stat["total"]
    39→    return rates
    40→
    41→
    42→def _auto_fixer_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    43→    auto_fix_actions = [
    44→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    45→    ]
    46→    if not auto_fix_actions:
    47→        return []
    48→    total = sum(action.get("count", 0) for action in auto_fix_actions)
    49→    if total <= 0:
    50→        return []
    51→    first_cmd = auto_fix_actions[0].get("command", "desloppify autofix <fixer> --dry-run")
    52→    return [
    53→        {
    54→            "type": "auto_fixers_available",
    55→            "message": f"{total} issues are auto-fixable. Run `{first_cmd}`.",
    56→            "command": first_cmd,
    57→        }
    58→    ]
    59→
    60→
    61→def _rescan_needed_reminder(command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    62→    if command not in {"autofix", "resolve", "suppress"}:
    63→        return []
    64→    return [
    65→        {
    66→            "type": "rescan_needed",
    67→            "message": "Rescan to verify — cascading effects may create new issues.",
    68→            "command": "desloppify scan",
    69→        }
    70→    ]
    71→

    73→def _badge_reminder(strict_score: float | None, badge: dict) -> list[dict]:
    74→    eligible_for_badge = (
    75→        strict_score is not None
    76→        and strict_score >= 90
    77→        and badge.get("generated")
    78→        and not badge.get("in_readme")
    79→    )
    80→    if not eligible_for_badge:
    81→        return []
    82→    badge_path = str(badge.get("path") or "scorecard.png")
    83→    return [
    84→        {
    85→            "type": "badge_recommendation",
    86→            "message": (
    87→                "Score is above 90! Add the scorecard to your README: "
    88→                f'<img src="{badge_path}" width="100%">'
    89→            ),
    90→            "command": None,
    91→        }
    92→    ]

    95→def _wontfix_debt_reminders(state: StateModel, debt: dict, command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    96→    reminders: list[dict] = []
    97→    if debt.get("trend") == "growing":
    98→        reminders.append(
    99→            {
   100→                "type": "wontfix_growing",
   101→                "message": "Wontfix debt is growing. Review stale decisions: `desloppify show --status wontfix`.",
   102→                "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   103→            }
   104→        )
   105→
   106→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   107→    if scan_count < 20 or command != "scan":
   108→        return reminders
   109→
   110→    stale_wontfix = []
   111→    for issue in state.get("issues", {}).values():
   112→        if issue.get("status") != "wontfix":
   113→            continue
   114→        resolved_at = issue.get("resolved_at")
   115→        if not resolved_at:
   116→            continue
   117→        try:
   118→            age_days = (_dt.now(UTC) - _dt.fromisoformat(resolved_at)).days
   119→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   120→            log_best_effort_failure(
   121→                logger, f"parse wontfix timestamp {resolved_at!r}", exc
   122→            )
   123→            continue
   124→        if age_days > 60:
   125→            stale_wontfix.append(issue)
   126→
   127→    if stale_wontfix:
   128→        reminders.append(
   129→            {
   130→                "type": "wontfix_stale",
   131→                "message": (
   132→                    f"{len(stale_wontfix)} wontfix item(s) are >60 days old. "
   133→                    f"Has anything changed? Review with: "
   134→                    f'`desloppify show "*" --status wontfix`'
   135→                ),
   136→                "command": 'desloppify show "*" --status wontfix',
   137→            }
   138→        )
   139→    return reminders
   140→

   142→def _ignore_suppression_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   143→    """Nudge when ignores/suppression are high enough to mask signal quality."""
   144→    integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {}) or {}
   145→    ignored = int(integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   146→    suppressed_pct = float(integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if ignored < 10 and suppressed_pct < 30.0:
   148→        return []
   149→    return [
   150→        {
   151→            "type": "ignore_suppression_high",
   152→            "message": (
   153→                f"Ignore suppression is high ({ignored} ignored, {suppressed_pct:.1f}% suppressed). "
   154→                "Revisit broad ignore patterns and resolve stale suppressions."
   155→            ),
   156→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   157→        }
   158→    ]
   159→

   161→def _stagnation_reminders(dimensions: dict) -> list[dict]:
   162→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   163→    for dim in dimensions.get("stagnant_dimensions", []):
   164→        strict = dim.get("strict", 0)
   165→        if strict >= 99:
   166→            message = (
   167→                f"{dim['name']} has been at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
   168→                f"The remaining items may be worth marking as wontfix if they're intentional."
   169→            )
   170→        else:
   171→            message = (
   172→                f"{dim['name']} has been stuck at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
   173→                f"Try tackling it from a different angle — run `desloppify next` to find the right entry point."
   174→            )
   175→        reminders.append(
   176→            {
   177→                "type": "stagnant_nudge",
   178→                "message": message,
   179→                "command": None,
   180→            }
   181→        )
   182→    return reminders

   185→def _dry_run_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:

   197→def _zone_classification_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   198→    zone_dist = state.get("zone_distribution")
   199→    if not zone_dist:
   200→        return []
   201→    non_prod = sum(value for key, value in zone_dist.items() if key != "production")
   202→    if non_prod <= 0:
   203→        return []
   204→    total = sum(zone_dist.values())
   205→    parts = [
   206→        f"{value} {key}"
   207→        for key, value in sorted(zone_dist.items())
   208→        if key != "production" and value > 0
   209→    ]
   210→    return [
   211→        {
   212→            "type": "zone_classification",
   213→            "message": (
   214→                f"{non_prod} of {total} files classified as non-production "
   215→                f"({', '.join(parts)}). "
   216→                f"Override with `desloppify zone set <file> production` "
   217→                f"if any are misclassified."
   218→            ),
   219→            "command": "desloppify zone show",
   220→        }
   221→    ]

   224→def _fp_calibration_reminders(fp_rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float]) -> list[dict]:
   225→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   226→    for (detector, zone), rate in fp_rates.items():
   227→        if rate <= 0.3:
   228→            continue
   229→        pct = round(rate * 100)
   230→        reminders.append(
   231→            {
   232→                "type": f"fp_calibration_{detector}_{zone}",
   233→                "message": (
   234→                    f"{pct}% of {detector} issues in {zone} zone are false positives. "
   235→                    f"Consider reviewing detection rules for {zone} files."
   236→                ),
   237→                "command": None,
   238→            }
   239→        )
   240→    return reminders

   243→def _review_queue_reminders(
   244→    state: StateModel,
   245→    scoped_issues: dict,
   246→    command: str | None,
   247→    strict_score: float | None,
   248→) -> list[dict]:
   249→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   250→    open_review = [
   251→        issue
   252→        for issue in scoped_issues.values()
   253→        if issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("detector") == "review"
   254→    ]
   255→    if open_review:
   256→        uninvestigated = [
   257→            issue
   258→            for issue in open_review
   259→            if not issue.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   260→        ]
   261→        if uninvestigated:
   262→            reminders.append(
   263→                {
   264→                    "type": "review_issues_pending",
   265→                    "message": f"{len(uninvestigated)} review issue(s) need investigation. "
   266→                    f"Run `desloppify show review --status open` to see the work queue.",
   267→                    "command": "desloppify show review --status open",
   268→                }
   269→            )
   270→
   271→    if command == "resolve" and state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   272→        reminders.append(
   273→            {
   274→                "type": "rereview_needed",
   275→                "message": "Subjective results may be stale after resolve. Re-run "
   276→                "`desloppify review --prepare` to refresh, or reset with "
   277→                "`desloppify scan --reset-subjective` before a clean rerun.",
   278→                "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   279→            }
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   283→    if not review_cache.get("files"):
   284→        current_strict = strict_score or 0
   285→        if current_strict >= 80:
   286→            reminders.append(
   287→                {
   288→                    "type": "review_not_run",
   289→                    "message": (
   290→                        "Mechanical checks look good! Run a subjective design review "
   291→                        "to catch issues linters miss: desloppify review --prepare"
   292→                    ),
   293→                    "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   294→                }
   295→            )
   296→
   297→    return reminders
   298→
   299→
   300→def _has_open_issues(state: StateModel) -> bool:
   301→    """True when any non-suppressed open issues remain in the queue."""
   302→    return any(
   303→        f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
   304→        for f in (state.get("issues") or {}).values()
   305→    )
   306→
   307→
   308→def _stale_assessment_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   309→    """Nudge when mechanical changes have staled subjective assessments."""
   310→    if _has_open_issues(state):
   311→        return []
   312→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
   313→    stale_dims = [
   314→        dim_key
   315→        for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items()
   316→        if isinstance(assessment, dict) and assessment.get("needs_review_refresh")
   317→    ]
   318→    if not stale_dims:
   319→        return []
   320→    dims_arg = ",".join(stale_dims[:10])
   321→    return [
   322→        {
   323→            "type": "stale_assessments",
   324→            "message": (
   325→                f"{len(stale_dims)} subjective dimension{'s' if len(stale_dims) != 1 else ''} "
   326→                f"stale after mechanical changes — re-review with: "
   327→                f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}`"
   328→            ),
   329→            "command": f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}",
   330→        }
   331→    ]
   332→
   333→
   334→def _review_staleness_reminder(state: StateModel, config: dict | None) -> list[dict]:
   335→    review_max_age = (config or {}).get("review_max_age_days", 30)
   336→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   337→    if review_max_age <= 0 or not review_cache.get("files"):
   338→        return []
   339→    try:
   340→        oldest_str = min(
   341→            review["reviewed_at"]
   342→            for review in review_cache["files"].values()
   343→            if review.get("reviewed_at")
   344→        )
   345→        oldest = _dt.fromisoformat(oldest_str)
   346→        age_days = (_dt.now(UTC) - oldest).days
   347→    except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   348→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "parse oldest review timestamp", exc)
   349→        return []
   350→    if age_days <= review_max_age:
   351→        return []
   352→    return [
   353→        {
   354→            "type": "review_stale",
   355→            "message": f"Design review is {age_days} days old — run: desloppify review --prepare",
   356→            "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   357→        }
   358→    ]
   359→
   360→
   361→def _feedback_reminder(
   362→    state: StateModel,
   363→    phase: str,
   364→    command: str | None,
   365→    fp_rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float],
   366→) -> list[dict]:
   367→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   368→    if scan_count < 2 or command != "scan":
   369→        return []
   370→    high_fp_dets = [
   371→        detector for (detector, _zone), rate in fp_rates.items() if rate > 0.3
   372→    ]
   373→    if high_fp_dets:
   374→        nudge_msg = (
   375→            f"Some detectors have high false-positive rates ({', '.join(high_fp_dets)}). "
   376→            f"If patterns are being misclassified, file an issue at "
   377→            f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} with the file and expected behavior — "
   378→            f"it helps calibrate detection for everyone."
   379→        )
   380→    elif phase == "stagnation":
   381→        nudge_msg = (
   382→            f"Score has plateaued — if you suspect desloppify is missing patterns "
   383→            f"or not capturing something it should, file an issue at "
   384→            f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} describing what you expected. "
   385→            f"Gaps in detection are a common cause of stagnation."
   386→        )
   387→    else:
   388→        nudge_msg = (
   389→            f"If you notice patterns desloppify doesn't detect, false positives, "
   390→            f"or issues that seem off, file an issue at {_FEEDBACK_URL} — "
   391→            f"include the file and what you expected. It helps improve the tool."
   392→        )
   393→    return [
   394→        {
   395→            "type": "feedback_nudge",
   396→            "message": nudge_msg,
   397→            "command": None,
   398→        }
   399→    ]
   400→
   401→
   402→_REMINDER_METADATA: dict[str, tuple[int, str]] = {
   403→    # Highest urgency operational nudges.
   404→    "rescan_needed": (1, "high"),
   405→    "ignore_suppression_high": (1, "high"),
   406→    "review_issues_pending": (1, "high"),
   407→    "rereview_needed": (1, "high"),
   408→    # Medium urgency quality/process nudges.
   409→    "wontfix_growing": (2, "medium"),
   410→    "wontfix_stale": (2, "medium"),
   411→    "stagnant_nudge": (2, "medium"),
   412→    "review_stale": (2, "medium"),
   413→    "stale_assessments": (2, "medium"),
   414→    "auto_fixers_available": (2, "medium"),
   415→    "zone_classification": (2, "medium"),
   416→    "fp_calibration": (2, "medium"),
   417→    # Informational nudges.
   418→    "badge_recommendation": (3, "low"),
   419→    "dry_run_first": (3, "low"),
   420→    "feedback_nudge": (3, "low"),
   421→    "review_not_run": (3, "low"),
   422→    "report_scores": (3, "low"),
   423→}
   424→
   425→
   426→def _decorate_reminder_metadata(reminders: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   427→    """Attach stable priority/severity metadata and sort by priority."""
   428→    decorated: list[dict] = []
   429→    for reminder in reminders:
   430→        reminder_type = str(reminder.get("type", ""))
   431→        key = reminder_type
   432→        if reminder_type.startswith("fp_calibration_"):
   433→            key = "fp_calibration"
   434→        priority, severity = _REMINDER_METADATA.get(key, (3, "low"))
   435→        clone = dict(reminder)
   436→        clone.setdefault("priority", priority)
   437→        clone.setdefault("severity", severity)
   438→        decorated.append(clone)
   439→    decorated.sort(key=lambda item: (int(item.get("priority", 3)), str(item.get("type", ""))))
   440→    return decorated
   441→
   442→
   443→def _report_scores_reminder(command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   444→    if command != "scan":
   445→        return []
   446→    return [
   447→        {
   448→            "type": "report_scores",
   449→            "message": (
   450→                "ALWAYS share ALL scores with the user: overall, objective, and strict, "
   451→                "plus every dimension score (lenient + strict), including subjective dimensions. "
   452→                "The goal is to maximize strict scores."
   453→            ),
   454→            "command": None,
   455→            "no_decay": True,
   456→        }
   457→    ]
   458→
   459→
   460→def _apply_decay(
   461→    reminders: list[dict], reminder_history: dict
   462→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   463→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   464→    for reminder in reminders:
   465→        if reminder.get("no_decay"):
   466→            filtered.append(reminder)
   467→            continue
   468→        count = reminder_history.get(reminder["type"], 0)
   469→        if count < _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD:
   470→            filtered.append(reminder)
   471→
   472→    updated_history = dict(reminder_history)
   473→    for reminder in filtered:
   474→        updated_history[reminder["type"]] = updated_history.get(reminder["type"], 0) + 1
   475→    return filtered, updated_history
   476→
   477→
   478→def compute_reminders(
   479→    state: StateModel,
   480→    lang: str | None,
   481→    phase: str,
   482→    debt: dict,
   483→    actions: list[dict],
   484→    dimensions: dict,
   485→    badge: dict,
   486→    command: str | None,
   487→    config: dict | None = None,
   488→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   489→    """Compute context-specific reminders, suppressing those shown too many times."""
   490→    del lang  # Reserved for future language-specific rules.
   491→
   492→    strict_score = score_snapshot(state).strict
   493→    reminder_history = state.get("reminder_history", {})
   494→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(
   495→        state.get("issues", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   496→    )
   497→    fp_rates = _compute_fp_rates(scoped_issues)
   498→
   499→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   500→    reminders.extend(_report_scores_reminder(command))
   501→    reminders.extend(_auto_fixer_reminder(actions))
   502→    reminders.extend(_rescan_needed_reminder(command))
   503→    reminders.extend(_badge_reminder(strict_score, badge))
   504→    reminders.extend(_wontfix_debt_reminders(state, debt, command))
   505→    reminders.extend(_ignore_suppression_reminder(state))
   506→    reminders.extend(_stagnation_reminders(dimensions))
   507→    reminders.extend(_dry_run_reminder(actions))
   508→    reminders.extend(_zone_classification_reminder(state))
   509→    reminders.extend(_fp_calibration_reminders(fp_rates))
   510→    reminders.extend(
   511→        _review_queue_reminders(state, scoped_issues, command, strict_score)
   512→    )
   513→    reminders.extend(_stale_assessment_reminder(state))
   514→    reminders.extend(_review_staleness_reminder(state, config))
   515→    reminders.extend(_feedback_reminder(state, phase, command, fp_rates))
   516→
   517→    reminders = _decorate_reminder_metadata(reminders)
   518→    return _apply_decay(reminders, reminder_history)
   519→

1→"""Complexity-focused mechanical evidence clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from ._accessors import _get_detail, _get_signals, _safe_num
     8→
     9→
    10→def _build_complexity_hotspots(
    11→    by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]],
    12→    by_file: dict[str, list[dict]],
    13→) -> list[dict]:
    14→    """Top 20 files by composite complexity score."""
    15→    del by_file
    16→    file_data: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    17→
    18→    for issue in by_detector.get("structural", []):
    19→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    20→        if not filepath:
    21→            continue
    22→        signals = _get_signals(issue)
    23→        entry = file_data.setdefault(
    24→            filepath,
    25→            {
    26→                "file": filepath,
    27→                "loc": 0,
    28→                "complexity_score": 0,
    29→                "signals": [],
    30→                "component_count": 0,
    31→                "function_count": 0,
    32→                "monster_functions": 0,
    33→                "cyclomatic_hotspots": 0,
    34→            },
    35→        )
    36→        entry["loc"] = max(entry["loc"], _safe_num(signals.get("loc")))
    37→        entry["function_count"] = max(
    38→            entry["function_count"], _safe_num(signals.get("function_count"))
    39→        )
    40→        entry["component_count"] = max(
    41→            entry["component_count"], _safe_num(signals.get("component_count"))
    42→        )
    43→
    44→        max_params = _safe_num(signals.get("max_params"))
    45→        if max_params >= 5:
    46→            entry["signals"].append(f"{int(max_params)} params")
    47→        max_nesting = _safe_num(signals.get("max_nesting"))
    48→        if max_nesting >= 4:
    49→            entry["signals"].append(f"nesting depth {int(max_nesting)}")
    50→        complexity = _safe_num(signals.get("complexity_score"))
    51→        entry["complexity_score"] = max(entry["complexity_score"], complexity)
    52→
    53→    for issue in by_detector.get("smells", []):
    54→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    55→        smell_id = _get_detail(issue, "smell_id", "")
    56→        if filepath in file_data:
    57→            if smell_id == "monster_function":
    58→                file_data[filepath]["monster_functions"] += 1
    59→            elif smell_id in ("cyclomatic_complexity", "high_cyclomatic"):
    60→                file_data[filepath]["cyclomatic_hotspots"] += 1
    61→
    62→    for issue in by_detector.get("responsibility_cohesion", []):
    63→        filepath = issue.get("file", "")
    64→        if filepath in file_data:
    65→            clusters = _safe_num(_get_detail(issue, "cluster_count"))
    66→            file_data[filepath]["component_count"] = max(
    67→                file_data[filepath]["component_count"], clusters
    68→            )
    69→
    70→    for entry in file_data.values():
    71→        entry["_score"] = (
    72→            entry["loc"] / 100
    73→            + entry["complexity_score"]
    74→            + entry["component_count"] * 3
    75→            + entry["monster_functions"] * 10
    76→        )
    77→        entry["signals"] = list(dict.fromkeys(entry["signals"]))
    78→
    79→    ranked = sorted(file_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e["_score"])[:20]
    80→    for entry in ranked:
    81→        del entry["_score"]
    82→    return ranked
    83→
    84→
    85→__all__ = ["_build_complexity_hotspots"]
    86→

1→"""Structural-area reporting helpers for status output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_area
    10→
    11→
    12→def collect_structural_areas(
    13→    state: dict,
    14→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
    15→    """Collect T3/T4 structural issues grouped by area."""
    16→    issues = state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
    17→        state.get("issues", {}), state.get("scan_path")
    18→    )
    19→    structural = [
    20→        issue
    21→        for issue in issues.values()
    22→        if issue["tier"] in (3, 4) and issue["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
    23→    ]
    24→    if len(structural) < 5:
    25→        return None
    26→
    27→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    28→    for issue in structural:
    29→        area = get_area(str(issue.get("file", "")))
    30→        areas[area].append(issue)
    31→    if len(areas) < 2:
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda pair: -sum(f["tier"] for f in pair[1]))
    35→
    36→
    37→def build_area_rows(
    38→    sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]],
    39→    *,
    40→    max_areas: int = 15,
    41→) -> list[list[str]]:
    42→    """Build table rows from sorted area issues."""
    43→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    44→    for area, area_issues in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
    45→        t3 = sum(1 for issue in area_issues if issue["tier"] == 3)
    46→        t4 = sum(1 for issue in area_issues if issue["tier"] == 4)
    47→        open_count = sum(1 for issue in area_issues if issue["status"] == "open")
    48→        debt_count = sum(1 for issue in area_issues if issue["status"] == "wontfix")
    49→        weight = sum(issue["tier"] for issue in area_issues)
    50→        rows.append(
    51→            [
    52→                area,
    53→                str(len(area_issues)),
    54→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
    55→                str(open_count),
    56→                str(debt_count),
    57→                str(weight),
    58→            ]
    59→        )
    60→    return rows
    61→
    62→
    63→def render_area_workflow(
    64→    sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]],
    65→    *,
    66→    max_areas: int = 15,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    """Print overflow count and workflow instructions for structural work."""
    69→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
    70→    if remaining > 0:
    71→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
    72→
    73→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
    74→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan",
    78→            "dim",
    79→        )
    80→    )
    81→    print(
    82→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
    83→    )
    84→    print()
    85→
    86→
    87→__all__ = [
    88→    "build_area_rows",
    89→    "collect_structural_areas",
    90→    "render_area_workflow",
    91→]
    92→
    93→

1→"""Structural signal helpers shared across language phase implementations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import resolve_path
     8→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import count_lines
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    10→from desloppify.state import Issue, make_issue
    11→
    12→
    13→def add_structural_signal(structural: dict, file: str, signal: str, detail: dict):
    14→    """Add a complexity signal to the per-file structural dict.
    15→
    16→    Accumulates signals per file so they can be merged into tiered issues.
    17→    """
    18→    f = resolve_path(file)
    19→    structural.setdefault(f, {"signals": [], "detail": {}})
    20→    structural[f]["signals"].append(signal)
    21→    structural[f]["detail"].update(detail)
    22→
    23→
    24→def merge_structural_signals(
    25→    structural: dict,
    26→    stderr_fn,
    27→    *,
    28→    complexity_only_min: int = 35,
    29→) -> list[Issue]:
    30→    """Convert per-file structural signals into tiered issues.
    31→
    32→    3+ signals -> T4/high (needs decomposition).
    33→    1-2 signals -> T3/medium.
    34→    Complexity-only files (no large/god signals) need score >= complexity_only_min
    35→    to be flagged — lower complexity in small files is normal, not decomposition-worthy.
    36→    """
    37→    results = []
    38→    suppressed = 0
    39→    for filepath, data in structural.items():
    40→        if "loc" not in data["detail"]:
    41→            try:
    42→                p = (
    43→                    Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else get_project_root() / filepath
    44→                )
    45→                data["detail"]["loc"] = count_lines(p)
    46→            except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
    47→                data["detail"]["loc"] = 0
    48→
    49→        # Suppress complexity-only issues below the elevated threshold.
    50→        signals = data["signals"]
    51→        is_complexity_only = all(s.startswith("complexity") for s in signals)
    52→        if is_complexity_only:
    53→            score = data["detail"].get("complexity_score", 0)
    54→            if score < complexity_only_min:
    55→                suppressed += 1
    56→                continue
    57→
    58→        signal_count = len(signals)
    59→        tier = 4 if signal_count >= 3 else 3
    60→        confidence = "high" if signal_count >= 3 else "medium"
    61→        summary = "Needs decomposition: " + " / ".join(signals)
    62→        results.append(
    63→            make_issue(
    64→                "structural",
    65→                filepath,
    66→                "",
    67→                tier=tier,
    68→                confidence=confidence,
    69→                summary=summary,
    70→                detail=data["detail"],
    71→            )
    72→        )
    73→    if suppressed:
    74→        stderr_fn(
    75→            "         "
    76→            f"{suppressed} complexity-only files below threshold (< {complexity_only_min})"
    77→        )
    78→    stderr_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} structural issues")
    79→    return results
    80→
    81→
    82→__all__ = ["add_structural_signal", "merge_structural_signals"]
    83→

1→"""Issue factory functions — normalize raw detector output into Issue dicts."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
     9→from desloppify.base.enums import Tier
    10→from desloppify.state import Issue, make_issue
    11→
    12→
    13→def make_unused_issues(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Issue]:
    14→    """Transform raw unused-detector entries into normalized issues.
    15→
    16→    Shared by both Python and TypeScript unused phases.
    17→    """
    18→    results = []
    19→    for e in entries:
    20→        tier = 1 if e["category"] == "imports" else 2
    21→        results.append(
    22→            make_issue(
    23→                "unused",
    24→                e["file"],
    25→                e["name"],
    26→                tier=tier,
    27→                confidence="high",
    28→                summary=f"Unused {e['category']}: {e['name']}",
    29→                detail={"line": e["line"], "category": e["category"]},
    30→            )
    31→        )
    32→    stderr_fn(f"         {len(entries)} instances -> {len(results)} issues")
    33→    return results
    34→
    35→
    36→def make_dupe_issues(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Issue]:
    37→    """Transform clustered duplicate entries into normalized issues.
    38→
    39→    Each entry represents a cluster of similar functions. One issue per cluster.
    40→    """
    41→    results = []
    42→    for e in entries:
    43→        a, b = e["fn_a"], e["fn_b"]
    44→        if a["loc"] < 10 and b["loc"] < 10:
    45→            continue
    46→        cluster_size = e.get("cluster_size", 2)
    47→        pair = sorted([(a["file"], a["name"]), (b["file"], b["name"])])
    48→        name = f"{pair[0][1]}::{rel(pair[1][0])}::{pair[1][1]}"
    49→        tier = 2 if e["kind"] == "exact" else 3
    50→        conf = "high" if e["kind"] == "exact" else "low"
    51→        kind_label = "Exact" if e["kind"] == "exact" else "Near"
    52→        if cluster_size > 2:
    53→            summary = (
    54→                f"{kind_label} dupe cluster ({cluster_size} functions, "
    55→                f"{e['similarity']:.0%} similar): "
    56→                f"{a['name']} ({rel(a['file'])}:{a['line']}), "
    57→                f"{b['name']} ({rel(b['file'])}:{b['line']}), ..."
    58→            )
    59→        else:
    60→            summary = (
    61→                f"{kind_label} dupe: "
    62→                f"{a['name']} ({rel(a['file'])}:{a['line']}) <-> "
    63→                f"{b['name']} ({rel(b['file'])}:{b['line']}) [{e['similarity']:.0%}]"
    64→            )
    65→        results.append(
    66→            make_issue(
    67→                "dupes",
    68→                pair[0][0],
    69→                name,
    70→                tier=tier,
    71→                confidence=conf,
    72→                summary=summary,
    73→                detail={
    74→                    "fn_a": a,
    75→                    "fn_b": b,
    76→                    "similarity": e["similarity"],
    77→                    "kind": e["kind"],
    78→                    "cluster_size": cluster_size,
    79→                    "cluster": e.get("cluster", [a, b]),
    80→                },
    81→            )
    82→        )
    83→    suppressed = sum(
    84→        1 for e in entries if e["fn_a"]["loc"] < 10 and e["fn_b"]["loc"] < 10
    85→    )
    86→    stderr_fn(f"         {len(entries)} clusters, {suppressed} suppressed (<10 LOC)")
    87→    return results
    88→
    89→
    90→def make_single_use_issues(
    91→    entries: list[dict],
    92→    get_area,
    93→    *,
    94→    loc_range: tuple[int, int] = (50, 200),
    95→    suppress_colocated: bool = True,
    96→    skip_dir_names: set[str] | None = None,
    97→    stderr_fn,
    98→) -> list[Issue]:
    99→    """Filter and normalize single-use entries into issues.
   100→
   101→    Suppresses entries within the LOC range (they're appropriately-sized abstractions),
   102→    entries co-located with their sole importer, and entries in skip_dir_names
   103→    directories (e.g., commands/ -- CLI modules are single-use by design).
   104→    """
   105→    results = []
   106→    colocated_suppressed = 0
   107→    lo, hi = loc_range
   108→    for e in entries:
   109→        if lo <= e["loc"] <= hi:
   110→            continue
   111→        # Skip files in directories that are single-use by design (e.g., commands/)
   112→        if skip_dir_names:
   113→            parts = Path(e["file"]).parts
   114→            if any(p in skip_dir_names for p in parts):
   115→                continue
   116→        if suppress_colocated and get_area:
   117→            src_area = get_area(rel(e["file"]))
   118→            imp_area = get_area(e["sole_importer"])
   119→            if src_area == imp_area:
   120→                colocated_suppressed += 1
   121→                continue
   122→        results.append(
   123→            make_issue(
   124→                "single_use",
   125→                e["file"],
   126→                "",
   127→                tier=3,
   128→                confidence="medium",
   129→                summary=f"Single-use ({e['loc']} LOC): only imported by {e['sole_importer']}",
   130→                detail={"loc": e["loc"], "sole_importer": e["sole_importer"]},
   131→            )
   132→        )
   133→    suppressed = len(entries) - len(results)
   134→    coloc_note = f", {colocated_suppressed} co-located" if colocated_suppressed else ""
   135→    stderr_fn(
   136→        f"         single-use: {len(entries)} found, {suppressed} suppressed "
   137→        f"({lo}-{hi} LOC{coloc_note})"
   138→    )
   139→    return results
   140→
   141→
   142→def make_cycle_issues(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Issue]:
   143→    """Normalize import cycles into issues."""
   144→    results = []
   145→    for cy in entries:
   146→        cycle_files = [rel(f) for f in cy["files"]]
   147→        name = "::".join(cycle_files[:4])
   148→        if len(cycle_files) > 4:
   149→            name += f"::+{len(cycle_files) - 4}"
   150→        tier = 3 if cy["length"] <= 3 else 4
   151→        results.append(
   152→            make_issue(
   153→                "cycles",
   154→                cy["files"][0],
   155→                name,
   156→                tier=tier,
   157→                confidence="high",
   158→                summary=f"Import cycle ({cy['length']} files): "
   159→                + " -> ".join(cycle_files[:5])
   160→                + (f" -> +{len(cycle_files) - 5}" if len(cycle_files) > 5 else ""),
   161→                detail={"files": cycle_files, "length": cy["length"]},
   162→            )
   163→        )
   164→    if entries:
   165→        stderr_fn(f"         cycles: {len(entries)} import cycles")
   166→    return results
   167→
   168→
   169→def make_orphaned_issues(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Issue]:
   170→    """Normalize orphaned file entries into issues."""
   171→    results = []
   172→    for e in entries:
   173→        results.append(
   174→            make_issue(
   175→                "orphaned",
   176→                e["file"],
   177→                "",
   178→                tier=3,
   179→                confidence="medium",
   180→                summary=f"Orphaned file ({e['loc']} LOC): zero importers, not an entry point",
   181→                detail={"loc": e["loc"]},
   182→            )
   183→        )
   184→    if entries:
   185→        stderr_fn(f"         orphaned: {len(entries)} files with zero importers")
   186→    return results
   187→
   188→
   189→SMELL_TIER_MAP = {"high": Tier.QUICK_FIX, "medium": Tier.JUDGMENT, "low": Tier.JUDGMENT}
   190→
   191→
   192→def make_smell_issues(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Issue]:
   193→    """Group smell entries by file and assign tiers from severity.
   194→
   195→    Input: list of smell dicts from detect_smells, each with id/label/severity/matches.
   196→    Output: issues grouped per (file, smell_id).
   197→    """
   198→    results = []
   199→    for e in entries:
   200→        by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   201→        for m in e["matches"]:
   202→            by_file[m["file"]].append(m)
   203→        for file, matches in by_file.items():
   204→            conf = "medium" if e["severity"] != "low" else "low"
   205→            tier = SMELL_TIER_MAP.get(e["severity"], 3)
   206→            results.append(
   207→                make_issue(
   208→                    "smells",
   209→                    file,
   210→                    e["id"],
   211→                    tier=tier,
   212→                    confidence=conf,
   213→                    summary=f"{len(matches)}x {e['label']}",
   214→                    detail={
   215→                        "smell_id": e["id"],
   216→                        "severity": e["severity"],
   217→                        "count": len(matches),
   218→                        "lines": [m["line"] for m in matches[:10]],
   219→                    },
   220→                )
   221→            )
   222→    stderr_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} smell issues")
   223→    return results
   224→
   225→
   226→def make_passthrough_issues(
   227→    entries: list[dict],
   228→    name_key: str,
   229→    total_key: str,
   230→    stderr_fn,
   231→) -> list[Issue]:
   232→    """Normalize passthrough detection results into issues."""
   233→    results = []
   234→    for e in entries:
   235→        label = e[name_key]
   236→        results.append(
   237→            make_issue(
   238→                "props",
   239→                e["file"],
   240→                f"passthrough::{label}",
   241→                tier=e["tier"],
   242→                confidence=e["confidence"],
   243→                summary=f"Passthrough: {label} "
   244→                f"({e['passthrough']}/{e[total_key]} forwarded, {e['ratio']:.0%})",
   245→                detail={k: v for k, v in e.items() if k != "file"},
   246→            )
   247→        )
   248→    if entries:
   249→        stderr_fn(f"         passthrough: {len(entries)} issues")
   250→    return results
   251→
   252→
   253→def make_facade_issues(entries: list[dict], stderr_fn) -> list[Issue]:
   254→    """Normalize re-export facade entries into issues."""
   255→    results = []
   256→    for e in entries:
   257→        kind = e["kind"]
   258→        if kind == "directory":
   259→            summary = (
   260→                f"Facade directory ({e['loc']} LOC, {e.get('file_count', '?')} files): "
   261→                f"all modules are re-exports ({e['importers']} importers)"
   262→            )
   263→        else:
   264→            from_str = ", ".join(e["imports_from"][:3])
   265→            if len(e["imports_from"]) > 3:
   266→                from_str += f", +{len(e['imports_from']) - 3}"
   267→            summary = (
   268→                f"Re-export facade ({e['loc']} LOC): "
   269→                f"imports from {from_str} ({e['importers']} importers)"
   270→            )
   271→        results.append(
   272→            make_issue(
   273→                "facade",
   274→                e["file"],
   275→                "",
   276→                tier=2,
   277→                confidence="high" if e["importers"] == 0 else "medium",
   278→                summary=summary,
   279→                detail={
   280→                    "loc": e["loc"],
   281→                    "importers": e["importers"],
   282→                    "imports_from": e["imports_from"],
   283→                    "kind": kind,
   284→                },
   285→            )
   286→        )
   287→    if entries:
   288→        stderr_fn(f"         facades: {len(entries)} re-export facade issues")
   289→    return results
   290→

1→"""Action computation engine used by narrative query generation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import merge_potentials
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.core import (
    10→    compute_score_impact,
    11→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
    14→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionContext, ActionItem
    15→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    17→
    18→
    19→def supported_fixers(state: StateModel, lang: str | None) -> set[str] | None:
    20→    """Return supported fixers for the active language, or None when unknown."""
    21→    if not lang:
    22→        return None
    23→
    24→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities", {}).get(lang, {})
    25→    fixers = capabilities.get("fixers")
    26→    if isinstance(fixers, list):
    27→        return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
    28→
    29→    try:
    30→        return set(get_lang(lang).fixers.keys())
    31→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→
    35→def _impact_calculator(
    36→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    37→    state: StateModel,
    38→) -> Callable[[str, int], float]:
    39→    """Build an impact estimator closure keyed by detector and count."""
    40→    merged_potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    41→    if not merged_potentials or not dimension_scores:
    42→        return lambda _detector, _count: 0.0
    43→
    44→    scoring_view = {
    45→        name: {
    46→            "score": values["score"],
    47→            "tier": values.get("tier", 3),
    48→            "detectors": values.get("detectors", {}),
    49→        }
    50→        for name, values in dimension_scores.items()
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    def _impact(detector: str, count: int) -> float:
    54→        return compute_score_impact(
    55→            scoring_view, merged_potentials, detector, count
    56→        )
    57→
    58→    return _impact
    59→
    60→
    61→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    62→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    63→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    64→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    65→
    66→
    67→def _fixer_has_applicable_issues(
    68→    state: StateModel, detector: str, fixer_name: str
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """For the smells detector, verify the fixer has matching open issues.
    71→
    72→    The smells detector aggregates many smell types but each fixer only handles
    73→    one sub-type (e.g. ``dead-useeffect`` only fixes ``dead_useeffect`` smells).
    74→    Without this check, a React-specific fixer can be suggested to projects that
    75→    have no React code at all, producing a confusing "Found 0 candidates" result.
    76→
    77→    For all other detectors the fixer is considered universally applicable.
    78→    """
    79→    if detector != "smells":
    80→        return True
    81→    smell_id = fixer_name.replace("-", "_")
    82→    return any(
    83→        f.get("status") == "open"
    84→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    85→        and f.get("detector") == "smells"
    86→        and f.get("detail", {}).get("smell_id") == smell_id
    87→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _append_auto_fix_actions(
    92→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    93→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    94→    supported: set[str] | None,
    95→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    96→    state: StateModel,
    97→) -> None:
    98→    """Append auto-fix/manual-fix actions for detectors with auto fixers."""
    99→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   100→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
   101→            continue
   102→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   103→        if count == 0:
   104→            continue
   105→
   106→        impact = round(impact_for(detector, count), 1)
   107→        available_fixers = [
   108→            fixer
   109→            for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]
   110→            if (supported is None or fixer in supported)
   111→            and _fixer_has_applicable_issues(state, detector, fixer)
   112→        ]
   113→        if not available_fixers:
   114→            actions.append(
   115→                {
   116→                    "type": "manual_fix",
   117→                    "detector": detector,
   118→                    "count": count,
   119→                    "description": (
   120→                        f"{count} {detector} issues — inspect with "
   121→                        f"`desloppify next` and fix manually"
   122→                    ),
   123→                    "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   124→                    "impact": impact,
   125→                    "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   126→                }
   127→            )
   128→            continue
   129→
   130→        fixer = available_fixers[0]
   131→        actions.append(
   132→            {
   133→                "type": "auto_fix",
   134→                "detector": detector,
   135→                "count": count,
   136→                "description": (
   137→                    f"{count} {detector} issues — run "
   138→                    f"`desloppify autofix {fixer} --dry-run` to preview, then apply"
   139→                ),
   140→                "command": f"desloppify autofix {fixer} --dry-run",
   141→                "impact": impact,
   142→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   143→            }
   144→        )
   145→
   146→
   147→def _append_reorganize_actions(
   148→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   149→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   150→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],

   158→            continue
   159→
   160→        guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "restructure with move")
   161→        actions.append(
   162→            {
   163→                "type": "reorganize",
   164→                "detector": detector,
   165→                "count": count,
   166→                "description": f"{count} {detector} issues — {guidance}",
   167→                "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   168→                "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   169→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   170→            }
   171→        )
   172→
   173→
   174→def _build_refactor_entry(
   175→    detector: str,
   176→    tool_info: dict[str, Any],
   177→    count: int,
   178→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   179→) -> ActionItem:
   180→    """Build one refactor/manual action row."""
   181→    guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "manual fix")
   182→    adjusted_info = {**tool_info, "guidance": guidance}
   183→
   184→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   185→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   186→        description = f"{count} files need design review — run holistic review to refresh subjective scores"
   187→    elif detector == "review":
   188→        command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   189→        suffix = "s" if count != 1 else ""
   190→        description = (
   191→            f"{count} review issue{suffix} need investigation — "
   192→            "run `desloppify show review --status open` to see them"
   193→        )
   194→        adjusted_info = {**adjusted_info, "action_type": "refactor"}
   195→    else:
   196→        command = f"desloppify show {detector} --status open"
   197→        description = f"{count} {detector} issues — {guidance}"
   198→
   199→    return {
   200→        "type": adjusted_info["action_type"],
   201→        "detector": detector,
   202→        "count": count,
   203→        "description": description,
   204→        "command": command,
   205→        "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   206→        "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def _append_refactor_actions(
   211→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   212→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   213→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   214→) -> None:
   215→    """Append refactor/manual actions after auto-fix/reorg buckets."""
   216→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   217→        if tool_info["action_type"] not in {"refactor", "manual_fix"}:
   218→            continue
   219→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   220→        if count == 0:
   221→            continue
   222→        actions.append(_build_refactor_entry(detector, tool_info, count, impact_for))
   223→
   224→
   225→def _append_debt_action(actions: list[ActionItem], debt: dict[str, float]) -> None:
   226→    """Append wontfix-debt callout when gap is material."""
   227→    gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   228→    if gap <= 2.0:
   229→        return
   230→    actions.append(
   231→        {
   232→            "type": "debt_review",
   233→            "detector": None,
   234→            "description": f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt — review stale decisions",
   235→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   236→            "gap": gap,
   237→        }
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→def _assign_priorities(actions: list[ActionItem]) -> list[ActionItem]:
   242→    """Sort and assign sequential priorities."""
   243→    type_order = {
   244→        "issue_queue": 0,
   245→        "auto_fix": 1,
   246→        "reorganize": 2,
   247→        "refactor": 3,
   248→        "manual_fix": 4,
   249→        "debt_review": 5,
   250→    }
   251→    actions.sort(
   252→        key=lambda action: (type_order.get(action["type"], 9), -action.get("impact", 0))
   253→    )
   254→    for index, action in enumerate(actions, start=1):
   255→        action["priority"] = index
   256→    return actions
   257→
   258→
   259→def _cluster_detector(cluster: dict) -> str | None:
   260→    """Extract the primary detector from a cluster.
   261→
   262→    Uses the cluster_key prefix (e.g. "auto::unused" → "unused",
   263→    "typed::dict_keys::phantom_read" → "dict_keys") or falls back
   264→    to the first member's detector.
   265→    """
   266→    key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   267→    if key:
   268→        parts = key.split("::")
   269→        if len(parts) >= 2:
   270→            return parts[1]
   271→    # Fallback: parse from cluster name (auto/detector-subtype)
   272→    name = cluster.get("name", "")
   273→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   274→        rest = name[5:]
   275→        return rest.split("-", 1)[0] if "-" in rest else rest
   276→    return None
   277→
   278→
   279→def _annotate_with_clusters(
   280→    actions: list[ActionItem], clusters: dict | None
   281→) -> None:
   282→    """Annotate actions with matching cluster info when clusters exist."""
   283→    if not clusters:
   284→        return
   285→    for action in actions:
   286→        detector = action.get("detector")
   287→        if not detector:
   288→            continue
   289→        matching = [
   290→            name
   291→            for name, c in clusters.items()
   292→            if c.get("auto") and _cluster_detector(c) == detector
   293→        ]
   294→        if matching:
   295→            action["clusters"] = matching
   296→            action["cluster_count"] = len(matching)
   297→            action["command"] = "desloppify next"
   298→            count = action.get("count", 0)
   299→            display = action.get("detector", "unknown")
